### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1155/1180 un-skip import-save journey: runAsync + DI bootstrap (TWO blockers) [Pattern + Bug-in-brief]
Un-skipped `test/views/import_recipe_journey_test.dart`'s save-persists test. The brief diagnosed ONLY a fake-async-settle problem (FakeFirestore writes on the REAL event loop, which `pumpAndSettle` doesn't drive) and prescribed `runAsync`. That was necessary but NOT sufficient — there was a SECOND, deeper blocker the brief missed:
- **`ImportBaseViewModel.saveImportedRecipe()` calls `_attachHeirloomIfPending()` → `ServiceLocator.get<HeirloomBridge>()` (fail-LOUD, not `tryGet`) BEFORE persisting.** The journey test never initialized DI, so the lookup threw "ServiceLocator not initialized"; `executeAsyncVoid` swallowed it → save returned `false` → the toast said "Sparande misslyckades", never "Recept sparat". Symptom was identical to a settle failure (toast absent), which is why the brief misattributed it. **Always read the SUT's save path for a fail-loud `ServiceLocator.get` before trusting a "just needs settle" diagnosis** — an unwired DI lookup and an unsettled async write both manifest as "the success UI never appears."
- **Fix:** add the canonical DI bootstrap to setUp — `await TestServiceLocator.initialize();` + `prod_locator.ServiceLocator.initialize(DIContainer());` (mirrors the sibling `url_import_viewmodel_test.dart` BUT-1181 fix). `TestServiceLocator.initialize()` registers a REAL empty `HeirloomBridge` in the shared GetIt; the prod `DIContainer` wraps that same GetIt so the lookup resolves and `_attachHeirloomIfPending` early-returns at `if (!bridge.hasPending) return;` (no pending draft). Pair with `await TestServiceLocator.reset()` in tearDown. setUp/tearDown must become `async`.
- **runAsync recipe for FakeFirestore-settle (the brief's part, confirmed correct):** `await tester.tap(saveBtn); await tester.runAsync(() async { await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50)); }); await tester.pump();` then assert the toast. The subsequent `firestore.collection(...).get()` is ALSO real async — wrap it in its OWN `runAsync`, capture `snapshot.data()` into a `late final Map<String,dynamic>?` declared outside, assert on the captured copy AFTER the runAsync returns (you can't `expect` the snapshot fields outside the runAsync because the read only completes inside it).
- Result: both tests green (`+2`, no `~`), assertions NOT weakened (toast + persisted doc's edited title `'Mina pannkakor'` + sourceUrl all still asserted). `dart analyze` clean.

### 2026-05-29 — AlgoliaSearchRepository BUT-1130 seam landed [Coverage + Bug]
The `withClient(...)` seam (abstract `AlgoliaClient` + pass-through) was implemented. `_FakeAlgoliaClient implements AlgoliaClient` with concrete recording bodies = the correct Fake spy template for any thin-SDK-wrapper seam. Filled gaps: `indexUser`/`removeUser` (route to USERS index + rethrow), `searchUsers` field mapping, `getSuggestions` index fork, `healthCheck` (must NOT rethrow). **LATENT BUG: `batchIndexRecipes` does NOT chunk** (one `batch()` for all recipes; full-library reindex over the ceiling partial-fails). Did NOT pin the buggy single-batch (would lock the bug); reported for a chunk-at-≤1000 fix.

### 2026-05-29 — errorStream side-channel getters need both branches (BUT-1112) [Pattern]
Any new `Stream<X> get foo => svc?.foo ?? const Stream.empty()` is TWO behaviors — forward + fallback. Test both or the fallback rots. The empty-stream test asserts `forEach` completes without emitting (catches removal of `?? const Stream.empty()` → NPE). `MockRealtimeSyncService.setError(e)` pumps onto `errorStream` — use it, not `when(() => mock.errorStream)`.

### 2026-05-29 — Mock-as-Fake (MockRealtimeSyncService) [Pattern]
`MockRealtimeSyncService extends Mock` carries concrete `@override` bodies (the BUT-368/369 anti-pattern — concrete bodies block `when()`) — pre-existing + consistent = effectively a hand-rolled fake; acceptable because callers drive it via setters. Don't add `when(() => mock.errorStream)`; stub via `setError`. Flag for an `extends Fake` rename if it grows.

### 2026-05-29 — TierResult.success overrides quality [Pattern]
`TierResult.success({recipe})` computes `quality: recipe.overallQuality` — it doesn't take a quality arg; a helper mapping `q` only to a confidence BUCKET means requested 0.65 → actual 0.70. **A test claiming to pin a threshold boundary is NOT testing the edge — a `>=`→`>` regression survives.** When a stub's "quality" is recomputed downstream, assert the boundary against the COMPUTED value, or build a recipe whose `overallQuality` lands exactly on the edge.

### 2026-05-29 — `if (x != null) expect(...)` guards are weakened assertions [Pattern]
A conditional `expect` is a silent skip: if you can PROVE the guarded branch always runs (probe empirically), drop the guard and assert unconditionally — else a regression making the value null turns the test GREEN. Also flagged a tautology `expect(a==null || b==false || b, isTrue)` (always true; proves only "no throw").

### 2026-05-29 — BUT-504 mocked-away subject (High) [Bug found]
**Inline-fake-as-subject smell: the test defined a PARALLEL service in the test file and tested THAT — never imported/instantiated the production manager** (200+ green lines proving nothing — "mocks away the behavior it claims to verify"). Fix: drive the REAL manager through a `FakeRepository implements ...` backed by `FakeFirebaseFirestore` (reads return real `QueryDocumentSnapshot`s; the Fake strips `serverTimestamp()` before set). **Repository extraction done RIGHT = swap the constructor arg, keep the behavior assertions.**

### 2026-05-29 — Boolean-gate branch coverage (AND vs OR) [Pattern]
**For a compound gate (`A && B`), both-true and both-false do NOT distinguish `&&` from `||`** — a `&&`→`||` mutation survives unless a single-axis case (exactly one side true) is pinned. Concrete: an instructions-only Swedish page (`ingredients=0, instructions=6`) passes the `&&` gate; an ingredients-only page parses empty/empty — use the instructions-only shape (verified by mutation: only the new test goes red).

### 2026-05-29 — BUT-1135 doc-only notification contract [Pattern]
**When a service documents "intentionally NOT a ChangeNotifier / read-after-await", the ONLY in-flight observable is `isLoading`** — a test that `await`s then reads post-state is a no-op duplicate. Teeth-bearing test asserts `isLoading == true` SYNCHRONOUSLY after the call starts AND from inside the unresolved repo Future (via a `Completer` gate + an in-flight hook on the fake repo) — fails if a refactor moves `_isLoading = true` after the await.

### 2026-05-29 — BUT-1164 mapper + BUT-472 dispose leak [Pattern]
**"Constant X must never resurface" guard**: assert the NEGATIVE (`isNot(meatFish)`) across all group prefixes incl. `''` (survives a refactor accidentally re-adding an aggregate bucket). **Load-bearing precondition**: the dispose test's precondition reads the SAME private map the post-dispose assert reads. Weakness: a `conflict_timers_count==0` assert is vacuous (the map is only populated by a remote conflict the happy-path never triggers — zero before/after).

### 2026-05-29 — BUT-520 BaseViewModel disposed-guard test [Pattern]
After `ChangeNotifier → BaseViewModel`, existing error-path asserts still cover the migrated code (public surface unchanged) — no weakening. But the disposed-guard benefit is untested unless a test drives an async op across `dispose()`. **Recipe (no `Future.delayed`):** stub the service to return a `Completer.future` gate; call the method; `dispose()`; THEN `gate.complete()` + `await` (completion lands on a disposed VM → exercises the guard; removing the override throws "used after disposed"). **Don't dispose the SHARED tearDown instance in a disposal test — create a LOCAL `disposableVm`.**

### 2026-05-29 — DI-seam: DISTINCT value, BOTH super-params [Pattern]
**Distinct-value discrimination**: register the locator-backed fake with value A, inject a second with value B, assert B (same-value passes even when the seam is broken). **Test each super-param independently** (two added together are a copy-paste hazard; a slip passes if only one is exercised). When a base captures `_x = ServiceLocator.get<X>()` AT CONSTRUCTION (was lazy), verify the locator is populated before construction.

### 2026-05-30 — tagsMutated + zero-allowlist arch guard (BUT-1055/1066) [Review]
tagsMutated tests gate the contract (`clearCache` BEFORE `controller.add`); "clears cache before firing" is strongest (a non-clearing invalidate makes the 2nd `getAllTags` a cache hit → both asserts fail). **`await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero)` flush IS sound** — broadcast `add()` schedules a microtask; `Duration.zero` is a Timer (macrotask) draining all pending microtasks first → deterministic; reusable for broadcast-stream flush, no fakeAsync. The zero-allowlist arch guard is NON-vacuous (scans 134 view files, asserts `isEmpty` over a populated set).

### 2026-05-30 — FriendsEmptyState a11y (BUT-975) [Weak-assertion catch]
**`expect(getSemantics(...), isNotNull)` is a NO-OP** (with `ensureSemantics()`, `getSemantics` walks to the nearest merged node, never null) — deleting `Semantics(button:true, identifier:...)` leaves it green. **Always pair `getSemantics` with `matchesSemantics(isButton:true, label:..., identifier:...)`.** `Image.asset` + `errorBuilder` + `pump()` (not `pumpAndSettle`) = safe (failed asset → fallback Icon, never throws).

### 2026-05-30 — HeirloomBridge ServiceLocator.get throws in unregistered suites (BUT-1154) [Pattern]
`saveImportedRecipe()` → `_attachHeirloomIfPending()` calls `ServiceLocator.get<HeirloomBridge>()` (deliberately `get` not `tryGet`); `HeirloomBridge` is intentionally not in `TestServiceLocator.initialize()` so unregistered suites throw → `executeAsyncVoid` swallows → `saved==false`. **When a shared base method grows a hard `ServiceLocator.get<T>()` (not `tryGet`), audit EVERY subclass suite that calls it — register T or it throws-and-returns-false silently.** Fix: register an empty `HeirloomBridge()` (`hasPending==false`); do NOT weaken `expect(saved, isTrue)`.

### 2026-05-30 — BUT-1154 photo-import decomposition commit-gate [Pattern]
A pure mixin-extraction move preserving the public surface needs NO new tests to merge — `flutter analyze` is the gate. **Test-harness-artifact ID (NOT production bugs):** failing tests (a) call the mock DIRECTLY then assert on `viewModel` state the test never mutated ("mocks away the behavior it claims to verify"); (b) the prod paths are covered by passing sibling happy-paths (a real bug breaks those too); (c) a leaky test-double shadow field diverges from the field the pipeline reads. Rewrite to drive the real pipeline or delete; don't weaken/skip to go green.

### 2026-05-30 — BUT-1170 AutoPersonalTagDisplay re-bind [Static-state widget test]
COMMIT-SAFE: intent-gated (the "Vegansk" assertion fails if `_onInstanceReady` re-bind is removed), authentic login/logout paths, static-state isolation sufficient (final `pumpWidget(SizedBox)` unmounts → resets all 5 statics; the ONLY test mounting this widget). **Placeholder-shimmer flush: custom `flush()` (pump + pump(50ms)) instead of `pumpAndSettle`** — an infinite shimmer never converges; reusable for any always-animating loading state. CAVEAT: the static `_instanceReadyController` is process-global, never closed — flag if a 2nd mounting test appears.

### CPI→LoadingIndicator migration (BUT-885/1066/1168/1173, iter-113 + waves 2-4) [Pattern]
Canonical rule for mechanical indeterminate `CircularProgressIndicator → LoadingIndicator` swaps inside static builders (subsumes the 2026-05-30, 2026-06-01 iter-113, and 2026-06-01 wave-2 entries; covers `lib/widgets/` social/common/image files + the `lib/views/` BUT-861 `StateWidget.loading()` siblings):

- **A pure indeterminate swap is behavior-preserving and needs NO new widget test.** `LoadingIndicator → AdaptiveActivityIndicator` stays on the indeterminate `CircularProgressIndicator` path on non-iOS hosts (the `flutter test` default) IFF `value == null && backgroundColor == null`. `valueColor → color` is the equivalent indeterminate swap. The regression contract is the arch guard (`architecture_test.dart` "no raw CircularProgressIndicator in lib/widgets|views/", BUT-885/1066) — **de-allowlisting the migrated file + confirming the guard still passes IS the proof**, plus `loading_indicator_test.dart` (pins value!=null → determinate CPI carrying value/strokeWidth/backgroundColor; value==null/.small → AdaptiveActivityIndicator). A `find.byType(LoadingIndicator)` re-assert = a low-value getter-identity test.
- The swap adds a net-new `Semantics(liveRegion: true)` node (a11y improvement, not a behavior change).
- **`find.byType(CircularProgressIndicator)` SURVIVES the swap on the default non-iOS host** — it only changes under `Platform.isIOS` (→ `CupertinoActivityIndicator`); a CPI finder breaks ONLY if a test both renders a migrated widget AND forces iOS — verify neither. Remaining CPI finders are in `test/widget/common/indicators/` (wrappers — correct) + tests rendering their OWN spinners; view tests use the wrapper finder or an OR fallback (`LoadingIndicator || CircularProgressIndicator`, BUT-891).
- **The ONE swap that DOES need an extra test: any `value:` fed by a ternary/conditional/nullable-cast is a BEHAVIOR BRANCH, not a swap** — grep the diff for `value:` on every migrated call site. Straight pass-throughs (`value: progress`) are covered by the widget's own null/non-null tests. But `value: status.progress > 0 ? status.progress : null` is a real domain branch (progress==0 must render INDETERMINATE, not a stuck 0%-full arc) — assess as new logic needing a boundary test (the widget's hardcoded-literal tests never exercise the `>0 ? : null` boundary). **Spinner type alone never deserves a behavioral test; a determinate/boundary `value:` clamp does.**

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-912 online-status privacy gate [Pattern]
Assessed coverage for a privacy opt-out (`showOnlineStatus` bool). Verdict: model round-trip + backward-compat-default + service gate cover the actual enforcement — adequate, not blocked. Reusable rules:
- **The load-bearing privacy-gate test pairs `verify(write false)` with `verifyNever(write true)`.** The gate is `effective = isOnline && showOnlineStatus` (service reads the *fetched* profile). The `false`-write leg alone passes even with the gate dropped UNLESS something else writes; the `verifyNever(true)` directly kills the dropped-gate regression. `user_service_test.dart:418` is the reference — this is the highest-value test of the feature and was done right.
- **Default-true bool round-trip must cover BOTH legs.** A serializer hard-coding `false` (or dropping the key → default-true read path) passes a `false`-only round-trip. Loop `[true,false]`. The backward-compat absent→true test covers the default but NOT present-`true`→true.
- **A `copyWith(x:false)` test asserting ONLY the flipped field is vacuous** — passes if some *other* field is collaterally clobbered. Add one "unchanged" assert (uid / a sibling bool) per the file's existing copyWith convention (`user_profile_test.dart:145`). These are strengthenings, not corrections; current tests aren't wrong, just loose.
- **"View-state convenience" ≠ trivial wiring when the setter branches asymmetrically.** `updateShowOnlineStatus(false)` ALSO clears `isOnline` (immediate dot removal: `copyWith(showOnlineStatus:false, isOnline:false)`), while the `true` branch only sets the flag. The service gate canNOT catch a regression that drops the `isOnline:false` clause (gate reads the fetched profile, not edited VM state) → genuine untested invariant. Pure-sync ~2-test block (off clears isOnline; on doesn't resurrect it) — cheap enough to add, but a "should" not a blocker. The view SwitchListTile IS trivial wiring (Phase 6: no view test).
- **Grep tool artifact**: some `//` comment lines render with a leading `\` in Grep output (saw it on `user_service.dart:291` + `user_profile_viewmodel.dart:104`) — VERIFY via Read before flagging a "syntax error"; the source files were clean.

### 2026-06-01 — BUT-1056 onShareError callback threading [Pattern]
**When a callback is threaded through N constructor hops, a direct-construction test of the LEAF proves the leaf FIRES it but never proves the WIRING.** The cap-rejection test constructs `RecipeSharingManager` directly with a local sink (intent-true: `newId==null` AND `surfacedError == errorShareCapReached(...)`). Real (low) gap: nothing proves `UnifiedRecipeService` passes `_setError` into `_socialContext`, nor that `SocialOperationsInitializer` (3 callsites) forwards `ctx.onShareError` through all branches — a refactor dropping it on one branch compiles clean + silently regresses with all 9 manager tests green. **Test the OUTERMOST wired unit you can cheaply build.** Design intent confirmed (NOT a gap): other return-null paths (recipe-not-found, empty createCollaborative) intentionally don't fire onShareError — only the cap path surfaces a dedicated message.

### 2026-06-15 — BUT-948 pantry multi-select: bulk-bar delete+undo wiring gap [Pattern + Gap]
**The classic stubbed-both-ends blind spot — here without the symmetry.** `pantry_viewmodel_bulk_test.dart` proves the VM's `bulkRemoveItems`/`restoreItems` contract (correct ids, no collateral delete, fresh ids on restore). `pantry_item_card_selection_test.dart` proves the card-level long-press/toggle/swipe-suppression contract. But `_PantryViewContent._deleteSelected` — the view method that (1) snapshots the removed items BEFORE the await, (2) calls `selection.clearSelection()` then `viewModel.bulkRemoveItems(ids)` in that order, (3) shows a `SnackBar` whose `onPressed` calls `viewModel.restoreItems(removed)` — is entirely untested at the widget level. Four distinct regressions are invisible to the current suite: (a) snapshot taken AFTER the await (items already gone, undo restores nothing); (b) `ScaffoldMessenger.maybeOf(context)` returns null in a host without a Scaffold (snackbar silently absent); (c) `onPressed` capturing a mutable reference instead of a snapshot (restores []); (d) `clearSelection()` not called before the delete (bar stays up during the async operation). This is P2 — the parallel with the existing swipe-undo widget test (`pantry_item_card_undo_test.dart`) is exact and that precedent exists precisely to catch wiring bugs like these. Closing test shape: pump `_PantryViewContent` (or a minimal `Stack(children:[...sections..., _PantryBulkBar(...)])`) with a mocked `PantryViewModel` and a real `PantrySelectionManager` pre-loaded with items; tap the delete button; assert `SnackBar` appears with "Ångra"; tap "Ångra"; `verify(() => mockVm.restoreItems(removed)).called(1)` where `removed` is the pre-seeded item list. **Note**: `_PantryBulkBar` is a private class — the test should either drive it through the `_PantryViewContent` integration or pump the bar widget directly via `find.byType` in a `tester.widget<_PantryBulkBar>(...)` call (not possible for a private class — drive at the `_PantryViewContent` seam). Separate observable: the single-item swipe undo test in `pantry_item_card_undo_test.dart` is fully behavioural and a good template. **Secondary observation**: `pantry_selection_manager_test.dart`'s `selectedIds is an unmodifiable view` test asserts the returned `Set`'s mutation throws `UnsupportedError` — an internal-representation detail; the observable safety property (callers can't corrupt state) would also be proven by asserting `selectedIds` after a caller-side mutate attempt shows no change in `selectedCount`/`isSelected`. Low priority, not wrong.

### 2026-06-02 — BUT-1162 ConflictBanner wiring into 3 surfaces [Pattern — gap closed]
**The iter-98 (entry 2026-05-28) gap "ConflictBanner widget ZERO test" is now CLOSED** — `test/widget/realtime/conflict_banner_test.dart` exists and is exemplary: renders-nothing-before-event, renders-localized-banner-on-event, `filterDocId` ignores-other-doc + surfaces-matching-doc, dismiss hides, `onViewChange` null→hidden / set→fires. Captures l10n via `Builder` (no hardcoded copy), drives the real `RealtimeSyncService.conflictStream` through a `StreamController` registered on the shared GetIt+prod-ServiceLocator bridge. **Therefore the per-surface BUT-1162 wiring (add import + place `ConflictBanner(filterDocId: docId)` at top of body / `SliverToBoxAdapter`-wrapped for a CustomScrollView) needs NO new test** — it's pure placement of an already-behavior-proven drop-in; a wiring test would only assert structure/topology (the BUT-368 anti-pattern). The one thing the widget test does NOT prove is "the right docId reaches `filterDocId`", but that's a 1-arg literal verifiable by reading the diff, not worth a test. **Regression risk to existing view tests is NIL**: `test/views/social/collaborative_shopping_view_test.dart` is the BUT-368 structural lane (`findsOneWidget` on `Scaffold`/`AppBar`/`LoadingStateBuilder`/`ChangeNotifierProvider<VM>`/`Consumer<VM>` + `CollaborativeShoppingView`) — none of those types are introduced by an *idle* ConflictBanner (it's `SizedBox.shrink()` until an event arrives, and even when active adds only `Material`/`Container`/`Row`/`Icon`, none asserted-on). No `find.byType(SizedBox/Material/Container/Row)` ASSERTIONS in that file (the two `SizedBox` hits are `child:` scaffolding). `menu_preview_view` and `edit_recipe_view` have NO widget tests at all. **General rule: wiring an already-tested self-subscribing collapse-to-nothing widget into a surface is placement, not behavior — test the widget once, never N× per host; the only real coverage gap to check is whether the host's existing tests assert a singleton count on a type the widget DOES introduce (here: none).**

### 2026-06-02 — BUT-1151 emulator-lane extraction of FakeFirestore-skipped tests [Pattern]
**Mechanical recipe for moving hard-`skip:`ped FieldValue tests off the fake onto the emulator lane** (when a repo's writer uses `FieldValue.arrayUnion`/`increment`/`serverTimestamp` that `MethodChannelFieldValue` vs `MockFieldValuePlatform` makes unrunnable under `FakeFirebaseFirestore`): create a sibling under `test/integration/firebase/<area>/` with `@Tags(['integration','firebase'])` + `library;` header, import `../../../test_support/emulator_lane.dart` (3-up from that depth), ONE top-level `group(...){...}, skip: emulatorOnlySkip);`, `late FirebaseFirestore firestore;` (interface type — the fake implements it so the file COMPILES + SKIPS cleanly on the mock tier and RUNS on the emulator tier), `setUp(() async { firestore = await firestoreForLane(); await clearLane(); ...build repo... })`. **The auth dep stays a `FakeAuthRepository` — only Firestore must be real.** Copy the constants + helper methods the moved tests use verbatim, rename every `fakeFirestore` → `firestore`, and drop each test's per-test `skip:` string (the group-level `emulatorOnlySkip` covers them). In the source unit file, replace each removed test with a one-line `// Note: ... moved to the emulator lane — see <integration file> (BUT-NNNN).` breadcrumb at the top of its (now-thinner) group, and remove any group emptied by the move. Verify: `dart analyze` clean on both; source still `+N` with the N-moved gone; new file on mock tier shows `+0 ~N` (all skipped, 0 run, 0 failures). **Gotcha — the `firebase` tag emits a `dart_test.yaml` warning** ("A tag was used that wasn't specified"): it's BENIGN and identical to the proven template (`notification_analytics_integration_test.dart`); do NOT add `firebase:` to `dart_test.yaml` if scope is "touch only these two files". **Keep direct-probe tests that DON'T call the FieldValue path in the unit file** (e.g. a raw `where().limit().get()` dedup-query probe) and annotate them with "Does NOT exercise the post-query consume branch — see <integration file> (emulator lane) for the fix-regression guard" so the split is documented at both ends. **Run wrapper on Windows: the `.bat` must put `C:\Windows\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0` on PATH** alongside `C:\tools\flutter\bin` — the flutter `dart`/`test` wrapper shells out to PowerShell and dies with "PowerShell executable not found" otherwise.

### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1180 scrollUntilVisible cross-OS flake [User correction + Pattern]
**`scrollUntilVisible(tile, delta, scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first)` is cross-OS flake bait whenever the view has MORE THAN ONE scrollable.** `.first` resolves by widget-tree order, not by which scrollable actually contains the target. In `create_shared_shopping_list_view`, the page-level `SingleChildScrollView` (the view body) precedes the friend list's own `ListView.builder` (inside `FriendCategoryManager`) in the tree, so `.first` grabbed the page scroll view → the friend-row scroll was a no-op. That no-op happened to leave the row hit-testable on Windows-local (taller render surface / different scroll physics) but timed out / `StateError`-ed on ubuntu+macos CI, reddening the whole `test/views` shard (the helper was load-bearing for 4 of 5 tests). **Fix: scope the scrollable to the widget that owns the target list** — `find.descendant(of: find.byType(<FriendListContainerWidget>), matching: find.byType(Scrollable)).first`. Verify the container's real type by reading the production view (here `FriendCategoryManager`, which holds a `ListView.builder` of the `CheckboxListTile` rows). **General rule for any multi-scrollable view test: NEVER `find.byType(Scrollable).first` — always descendant-scope from the list's owning widget, or `find.ancestor` from the target tile to its nearest Scrollable.** Lesson on verification scope: Windows-local green does NOT clear a CI views shard that runs on 3 OSes — treat any tree-order-dependent finder as suspect before it ships. Also documented: when a test installs a `FlutterError.onError` filter keyed on framework copy (`'A RenderFlex overflowed'`), add an inline comment noting the SDK-wording dependency so a future reword is traceable (the filter silently stops matching → the suppressed error resurfaces as a real failure, which is the desired fail-loud behaviour but otherwise looks mysterious).

### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1180 SharedWithMeView rebuild (mock-coordinator harness) [Pattern]
Rebuilt the @Skip'd ULTRATHINK `shared_with_me_view_test.dart` into 5 real state-rendering tests. Simpler than `create_shared` because the view has a SINGLE dep resolved via `ServiceLocator.get<SharedContentCoordinatorViewModel>()` in initState (NOT a self-built `ChangeNotifierProvider(create:)`) — so a **mocktail `MockSharedContentCoordinatorViewModel` (already in `production_mocks.dart`) registered via `TestServiceLocator.registerMock<SharedContentCoordinatorViewModel>(mock)` IS injectable** through the prod↔test bridge. Drive only the 4 getters `_buildContent` branches on: `isGloballyLoading`, `hasGlobalError`/`globalError`, `hasAnyContent`. **Key win: keep `hasAnyContent==false` for loading/empty/error states** — the SearchBar + TabBar sub-builders early-`return SizedBox.shrink()` when `!hasAnyContent`, so the loading/empty/error branches NEVER touch the 5 specialized child VMs (`searchViewModel`/`recipeViewModel`/etc.) → no child-VM stubbing needed. (A populated-state test WOULD need to stub all 3 content child VMs' `hasFilteredContent`/`totalCount`/`unreadCount` + `searchViewModel.hasSearchQuery` — deferred as heavier; the 3 user-visible degenerate states + 2 interactions are the high-value coverage.) Traps hit:
- **The view's top sliver `LayoutComponents.offlineIndicator()` (`_OfflineIndicatorState.initState`) hard-resolves `ServiceLocator.get<OfflineService>()` and reads `isOnline`** → unregistered = `GetIt ... OfflineService is not registered` StateError buried ~1000 frames deep in the CustomScrollView build. Register a `MockOfflineService` with `when(()=>svc.isOnline).thenReturn(true)` + void-stub `addListener`/`removeListener`. **General rule: a view whose scaffold/layout helper renders an offline/status indicator pulls in `OfflineService` even when the VM is fully mocked — register it in setUp.**
- **Mock coordinator's post-frame `initialize()` is awaited** → stub `thenAnswer((_) async {})` (bare Mock returns null → `_TypeError` on await). Also void-stub `addListener`/`removeListener`/`dispose` (ChangeNotifierProvider.value + the view's dispose call them) and `refreshAllContent` (the error-retry + app-bar refresh wire to it).
- **`pumpAndSettle` TIMES OUT on the loading state** (`LoadingIndicator` wraps a perpetual `CircularProgressIndicator` — same as the 2026-05-26 DialogFactory entry). Gate it: `pumpView(tester, settle:false)` → `pump()` + `pump(300ms)` instead of `pumpAndSettle()`; the empty/error states settle fine.
- Empty state asserts go through `StateWidget.empty(title/subtitle/actionLabel)` → `EmptyStates.buildEmptyState` renders all three as `Text` + a `primaryButton(label: actionLabel)`; tapping the CTA `Navigator.pushNamed(context, Routes.home)` (`'/'`) — verified with a tiny `_RouteRecorder extends NavigatorObserver` capturing `route.settings.name` (clear the list after the initial `'/'` home push, then assert `contains('/')`). Error retry verified via `verify(()=>coordinator.refreshAllContent()).called(1)`. Reused the `create_shared` scoped-`FlutterError.onError` 'A RenderFlex overflowed'-only filter (the scaffold+bottom-nav+centred illustration overflow vertically on a tall surface). Behaviors pinned: loading text+indicator, empty title+explanation+CTA, error message+retry, retry→refreshAllContent, CTA→home-route. Diff: only this one test file (+ `@Skip` removed); `dart analyze` clean; `+5 All tests passed`.

### 2026-06-01 — BUT-1171 PhotoImportViewModel @visibleForTesting setters [Pattern]
**Retiring a leaky test double: give production a single `@visibleForTesting` setter on the REAL field and delete the shadow field + getter overrides — do NOT add a parallel getter override.** `setOcrTextForTesting`/`setImageBytesForTesting` assign the real `_ocrText`/`_imageBytes` (+ `notifyListeners()`), so getters AND the pipeline observe the same state — this is the fix for the 2026-05-30 leaky-double finding (a shadow field diverging from the field the pipeline reads is the exact shape that produces "Expected true, Actual false" with zero production defect). The now-real save path is adequately covered (success / no-recipe-guard / save-failure, unblocked by the ServiceLocator bridge + empty `HeirloomBridge` in setUp). GENUINE GAP (file LOW): the heirloom-PENDING branch of `_attachHeirloomIfPending` is never exercised (bridge has no pending draft → early-returns); the whole BUT-953/1086/1161 upload sub-path (auth re-check → `errorAuthentication`, content-addressed path build, `uploadImageData`, draft-restore-on-failure) stays unpinned at the VM level — the new setters make a VM-level heirloom test cheap. Minor pre-existing smell: a `Future.delayed(10ms)` for processing-state (should be `fakeAsync`+`elapse`).

### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1180 FriendsListView (most service-entangled of the social set) [Pattern + Bug]
Rebuilt the ~50-test ULTRATHINK smoke-suite into 5 real behavior tests. Mirrors the `shared_with_me`/`create_shared` harness (prod bridge + `ViewTestHelpers.setupViewTestEnvironment` + `registerMock<T>` + plain localized MaterialApp). Six DI resolves in initState/build; the traps, in build-order so you can pre-wire all of them:
- **`MockSocialEventsTracker` (via `_NoOpFutureMock`) returns `Future<void>` for `logSocialOnboardingStartedIfFirstEntry`, which production declares `Future<bool>` → runtime cast fail in initState.** The shared no-op only special-cases the `logFirst\w+IfMilestone` regex; this `...IfFirstEntry` method slips to the `Future<void>` default. Fix WITHOUT touching production (parallel session): local `_FakeSocialEventsTracker extends Fake implements SocialEventsTracker` overriding just that method to return `Future<bool>`, + `_TestAnalyticsService extends MockAnalyticsService` overriding `social` to return it, `registerMock<AnalyticsService>(...)`. (Shared-mock gap worth a production follow-up: widen `_NoOpFutureMock` to cover `IfFirstEntry` too.)
- **`Provider.value(value: MockUnifiedFriendsService)` trips `Provider.debugCheckInvalidValueType`** — the mock mixes in `ChangeNotifier` (Listenable) while the real service isn't. `Provider.debugCheckInvalidValueType = null` in `setUpAll` (same as the create_shared entry).
- **TabBar Groups tab reads `friendsService.invitations.pendingReceivedInvitations`** — default `MockUnifiedFriendsService.invitations` returns a fresh unstubbed `MockFriendsInvitationsOperations` (noSuchMethod → null → crash on `.isNotEmpty`). Build one with `when(()=>ops.pendingReceivedInvitations).thenReturn(<GroupInvitation>[])` and pass via `setFriendsState(invitations: ops)`.
- **The view captures `FriendsViewModel` AND `ActivityFeedViewModel` in initState (both registered as FACTORIES) — override BOTH with singletons** via `registerMock`. The default tab is Feed (index 0) → `FeedTab.build` reads `ActivityFeedViewModel.isLoading`/`error` (BaseViewModel getters, noSuchMethod → null → cast-to-bool crash). Stub `isLoading`/`error` with `when()`. **DON'T stub `loadFeed`/`events`/`filteredEvents` — they're CONCRETE bodies on `MockActivityFeedViewModel`** → `when()` throws "No method stub was called from within when()". Drive `events` via `setFeedState(events: const [])` instead.
- **`FriendsViewModel` widget-mock only defines `friends`/`isLoading` concretely** — the view branches on `searchQuery`/`searchResults`/`incomingRequests`/`sentRequests`/`friendsCount` (all noSuchMethod) and `updateSearch` (returns `Future<void>`, stub `thenAnswer((_) async {})` NOT `thenReturn(null)`). The Find-friends tab (index 3, empty search) shows `RequestsTab` which reads BOTH `incomingRequests` AND `sentRequests` — miss `sentRequests` and you get `Null is not a subtype of List<FriendRequest>` only after the tab-switch, not at initial pump.

### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1180 CollaborativeShoppingView rebuild + the REAL re-entrancy root cause [Pattern + Bug]
Rebuilt the @Skip'd ~30-test ULTRATHINK smoke-suite (topology `findsOneWidget` on `ChangeNotifierProvider<VM>`/`Consumer<VM>` + Stopwatch "performance" asserts) into 5 real behavior tests. **The brief's premise that the 17 failures were a sync-StreamController re-entrancy was WRONG** — investigated empirically by running the unskipped suite. Actual root causes (two, neither is a re-entrancy):
- **(1) Type-mismatched `Provider.value` made the old "controlled VM" tests a no-op.** The old `TestableCollaborativeShoppingViewModel extends MockBaseViewModel` does NOT implement `CollaborativeShoppingViewModel`, so `ChangeNotifierProvider.value(value: testableVM)` registered the WRONG type — the view's `ChangeNotifierProvider(create: ...)` ALWAYS built its own production VM and the `.value` provider was dead. So every "controlled state" test was actually exercising the real VM against the default factory `MockUnifiedShoppingService`. There is no `BehaviorSubject`/sync controller in the test path — `MockUnifiedShoppingService.stateStream` is `.broadcast()` (emits nothing); the production `_stateSubject` (rxdart `BehaviorSubject`) is never constructed because the mock replaces the whole service. **The "Bad state: Cannot fire new event" in the @Skip note was misdiagnosed — the real failures were (2).**
- **(2) PRODUCTION LAYOUT FRAGILITY: the view shell's add-item Row goes horizontally-unbounded in a bare test Scaffold → `BoxConstraints forces an infinite width` from `FilledButton.icon` (`collaborative_shopping_actions.dart:173`).** Shell shape: `Scaffold > SafeArea > Center > ConstrainedBox(maxWidth: LayoutComponents.valueFor(mobile: double.infinity, ...)) > Column > [..., buildAddItemSection → Row[Expanded(TextField), FilledButton]]`. On the bare test harness the `Center`→`ConstrainedBox(maxWidth: infinity)` path leaves the Column (hence the Row) width-unbounded, so the non-flex `FilledButton.icon` is measured with infinite width and asserts. (In the shipped app a parent gives the Scaffold body a finite width, masking it — but it's a latent fragility: any host that hands this view an unbounded-width slot crashes. Reported, not "fixed" — parallel session owns production.) A SECONDARY framework assertion (`_RenderObjectSemantics.debugCheckForParentData` deep recursion + `Null check` in `_ViewportElement.debugVisitOnstageChildren`) cascades from the broken layout frame (the claimable items wrap in `Dismissible`); it disappears once the layout is sound.
- **The fix that keeps the tests faithful AND deterministic (group_detail template): drive the REAL production VM + REAL sub-widget, NOT the full shell.** Construct `CollaborativeShoppingViewModel(listId:, shoppingService: <our MockUnifiedShoppingService>)` directly (resolves `PermissionService`/presence from the bridge; `_presenceModule` is null because `ShoppingPresenceModule` isn't registered → presence skipped), `await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero)` to drain the ctor's fire-and-forget `_initialize()/_loadList()`, then render `CollaborativeShoppingItems(viewModel:, onToggleItem:)` inside a width-bounded `Scaffold(body: SizedBox(width: 600, ...))`. This pins the user-visible item behaviour (each item renders by Swedish name; empty list → "Inga varor ännu"; checkbox tap → `onToggleItem(id)`; bought item → strikethrough title) without depending on the fragile add-item Row.
- **Absent-list outcome is a VM-level test, not a widget test — because `executeAsync` RETHROWS.** `_loadList` throws on a missing list; `executeAsync` (StateNotifierMixin) does `setError(sanitizeErrorForUser(e))` THEN `rethrow`. The view's fire-and-forget `_initialize()` doesn't catch → the rejection surfaces as an uncaught zone error that fails ANY test that constructs the VM with the list already absent. **Recipe: construct with the list PRESENT (ctor `_initialize` succeeds, no uncaught rejection), drain the microtask, THEN remove the list and `await vm.refresh()` inside a `try/catch`** (refresh routes through the SAME `executeAsync(_loadList)` path) → assert `vm.hasError` + `vm.error == 'Ett fel uppstod. Försök igen.'` (the SANITIZED generic, NOT the raw `errorUnexpected` the throw carries — `sanitizeErrorForUser` collapses it).
- **Copy traps (assert exact ARB values):** `collaborativeNoItemsYet` = "Inga varor **ännu**" (not "...än"); `collaborativeListNotFound` = "**Lista** hittades inte" (not "Listan...") — and that not-found EMPTY state is unreachable when the list is absent because the ERROR branch (error != null) wins in `LoadingStateBuilder`. **`toggleItemBought(itemId)` takes ONE positional arg** (the old guess of two compile-fails). **`amount: 0` on `UnifiedShoppingItem` → `displayText == name`** (no amount/unit prefix) so `find.text('Mjölk')` works without a quantity. Diff: only this one test file (+ `@Skip` removed); `dart analyze` clean; `+5 All tests passed`. **Lesson: when a @Skip note names a root cause, RE-DERIVE it empirically — run the unskipped suite and read the first real exception — before designing around it; the note here blamed a re-entrancy that doesn't exist and the real blocker was a layout-constraint bug + a rethrow-out-of-fire-and-forget.**
- **Duplicate-copy collision**: with `friendsCount==0` the Feed tab's no-friends empty-state CTA `feedEmptyNoFriendsCta` is ALSO "Hitta vänner" — identical to the Find-friends TAB label. `find.text('Hitta vänner')` matches twice. Scope tab-label asserts to `find.descendant(of: find.byType(TabBar), matching: find.text(...))`.
Behaviors pinned: 4 Swedish tab labels + controller.length==4; Friends-empty branded state (headline + find-by-username CTA); empty-CTA → tab controller animates to index 3; Groups tab → create-group FAB appears (absent on Feed); social-flag OFF → hardcoded disabled fallback + no TabBar. Diff: only this one test file (+ `@Skip` removed); `dart analyze` clean; `+5 All tests passed`.

### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1185 PersonalTagSelectionManager [Pattern]
Pure-`ChangeNotifier` multi-select managers (`personal_tag_selection_manager.dart`, mirror of `recipe_selection_manager.dart`): zero deps, no `setUp` bootstrap beyond `addListener(()=>notifyCount++)`. **Pin the auto-exit invariant explicitly** — toggling the LAST selected id out sets `_isSelectionMode=false` (line 27-29); that's intended UI (empty selection bar is confusing), so pin-as-correct, don't flag. **`selectedTagIds` getter returns `Set.unmodifiable` → `expect(()=>ids.add('x'), throwsUnsupportedError)`** proves callers can't corrupt internal state. notifyCount==1 per mutation (no double-fire). Mirror sibling test for style — same group/intent structure, no `flutter_test` scaffolding needed.
### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1049 comment-image attachments [Pattern]
**`CommentFormWidget` cap-enforcement is testable through the existing harness**: the attach IconButton is gated `if (!_atImageCap)` (hidden, not disabled) once `_selectedImages.length >= RecipeComment.maxImageUrls`. Mock `picker.pickMultipleImages` to return `[tempImage]` each call, tap attach `maxImageUrls` times, assert `find.byIcon(Icons.add_photo_alternate_outlined)` goes `findsOneWidget`→`findsNothing`. The mock returning the SAME file each tap is fine — the cap counts entries, not uniqueness. **Upload-failure safety test is load-bearing and correct**: it pairs `errorShown==true` with `verifyNever(() => vm.postComment(...))` — proves no partial post when `uploadCommentImage` returns null (the "never publish a comment referencing images that didn't land" contract). Don't weaken to just the error-shown half.
**`CommentImageAttachments` renderer needs NO heavy DI** — pure StatelessWidget over a `List<String>`; only `context.l10n` (so the localized `_wrap` MaterialApp suffices, no ServiceLocator). `CachedNetworkImage` renders its placeholder synchronously in tests, so `findsNWidgets(N)` for N urls + `findsNothing` for `[]` (the `SizedBox.shrink` contract) work without network mocking. **The full-screen viewer (`_CommentImageViewer`) uses a `CircularProgressIndicator` placeholder → `pumpAndSettle` TIMES OUT** (recurrence of the 2026-05-26 DialogFactory CPI-pump rule); tap the thumbnail then `pump()` + `pump(Duration(milliseconds: 300))` and assert `find.byType(Dialog)`/`PageView`/`Icons.close`.
**VM→manager `imageUrls` threading is structural pass-through** (`postComment(id, {imageUrls}) => _commentsManager.postComment(id, imageUrls: imageUrls)`, no logic) — already pinned at the meaningful seam by the widget's "upload success: posts with the uploaded URLs" test (`verify(() => vm.postComment('r1', imageUrls: [url]))`). No separate VM test earns its keep; the viewmodel-test change was a Fake-signature update only. Renderer + form added 4 tests (1 cap + 3 renderer), full file now 7+3.

### 2026-06-03 — BUT-1185 PersonalTagSelectionManager [Pattern]
Pure-`ChangeNotifier` multi-select managers (`personal_tag_selection_manager.dart`, mirror of `recipe_selection_manager.dart`): zero deps, no `setUp` bootstrap beyond `addListener(()=>notifyCount++)`. **Pin the auto-exit invariant explicitly** — toggling the LAST selected id out sets `_isSelectionMode=false` (line 27-29); that's intended UI (empty selection bar is confusing), so pin-as-correct, don't flag. **`selectedTagIds` getter returns `Set.unmodifiable` → `expect(()=>ids.add('x'), throwsUnsupportedError)`** proves callers can't corrupt internal state. notifyCount==1 per mutation (no double-fire). Mirror sibling test for style — same group/intent structure, no `flutter_test` scaffolding needed.
**VM pass-through coverage rule**: `mergeTags`/`bulkDeleteTags` on `personal_tag_viewmodel.dart` (lines 277-283) are bare one-line `=> _service.x()` delegations — NO `safeExecute`, no state, no transform — already covered by `personal_tag_service_test.dart` (8 merge tests incl. same-id/empty-id edges). A VM test would only re-verify "delegation calls the method" = structural, skip it. **The real untested logic is the merge-LOOP in `personal_tag_bulk_dialogs.dart` (lines 88-119)**: filters out target, loops `mergeTags(fromId, target)` sequentially accumulating `updated`, partial-failure try/catch → snackbar. That loop + the "N>2 sequential merge is safe because source is deleted each call" assumption is a genuine coverage gap — but it lives in a dialog hitting `context.l10n`/`Navigator`/`SnackBarUtils`, so it's widget-test territory or In-Review manual pass, not a unit test.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1041 source-icon host-sniff helper [Pattern]
**`@visibleForTesting static IconData sourceIcon(String url)` is the RIGHT seam for a host→icon affordance** (`recipe_detail_shared_widgets.dart`). The host-sniff is a pure function; the Row/Flexible wrapping around it is layout that `buildTitleSection` only assembles after pulling UserService+ViewModel+Provider — exactly the heavy view-scaffolding ui-conventions.md says to skip. 8 unit tests pinning the host→icon contract (yt watch, youtu.be, m.youtube subdomain, tiktok, vm.tiktok, generic, hostless fallback, case) are adequate; do NOT demand a widget test of the Row. **Two cosmetic mislabels, NOT gaps (verified in Dart):** (1) `Uri.host` already lowercases per RFC 3986 normalization (`YouTube.com`→`youtube.com`), so production's `.toLowerCase()` is dead-defensive and the "case-insensitive" test passes with or without it — still a valid pin of user-visible behaviour, just not testing what its name implies. (2) `'not a url'` does NOT make `tryParse` return null — it parses to a relative-ref with empty host; the actual `tryParse→null` branch needs `'http://[invalid'`. Both the empty-host path and the null path land on `open_in_new` (identical user-visible result), and empty-host is already covered, so the unexercised `?? ''` null branch is a non-gap. **Verdict template for "pure helper extracted from a DI-heavy build method": cover the helper's contract at the helper, skip the widget assembly — and before claiming a missing edge case, run the input through `Uri.tryParse` in a scratch Dart file rather than assuming what parses to null vs empty.**

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-987 filter-panel manage-prefs deep-link [Pattern]
**Callback-gated contextual deep-link in a leaf StatelessWidget = exactly the right seam to test directly, NOT through the view.** `FiltersPanelWidget` is a pure `StatelessWidget` (no Provider/ServiceLocator); the new `if (onManageFoodPreferences != null) TextButton.icon(...)` render-gate + tap-wiring is the only logic the feature adds. 3 widget tests (renders-when-provided / tap-fires-callback / absent-when-null) via `createLocalizedTestApp(wrapInScrollView: true)` are adequate. The "absent-when-null" negative is load-bearing — pins the gate so a future refactor can't render a dead no-op button. **The MinaReceptView→SearchFilterWidget→FiltersPanelWidget threading is BUT-754 pass-through plumbing** (`onManageFoodPreferences: widget.onManageFoodPreferences`, no transform/conditional) — compile-time-checked named-param wiring, no own test earns its keep; the only unasserted bit is the `Routes.settingsAllergens` literal in the view closure (journey-test territory, skip per ui-conventions.md). **Latent brittleness flagged (NOT commit-blocking): the tests finder-key on `find.byIcon(Icons.tune)`, and `Icons.tune` is ALSO used by `FilterToggleButton`** — unique here ONLY because `FiltersPanelWidget` doesn't contain that toggle (it lives in the parent `SearchFilterWidget`). The robust finder is the localized label (`filterManageFoodPrefs` → "Hantera allergener & kost"), which keys on the feature's user-visible contract rather than a generic Material icon shared across the widget tree. **Pre-approve rule: when a widget test targets `find.byIcon(<genericMaterialIcon>)`, grep the package dir for other uses of that icon and confirm none co-render in the subject; prefer the localized label finder when the icon isn't feature-unique.**

### 2026-06-14 — RealtimeSyncService review: conflictStream gap CLOSED + dead-duplicate resolveConflict [Review + Gaps]
Re-reviewed `test/unit/services/realtime_sync_service_test.dart` against `lib/services/realtime_sync_service.dart` after iter commits 27c61e7aa (cache-coherence on conflict-loss) + d4ee51b0f (recoverLocalVersion gap-close). **The 2026-06-14 conflictStream entry above (line ~241, BUT-1031, "stubbed-both-ends blind spot") is now SUPERSEDED — the gap is CLOSED.** The `conflictStream (BUT-1265 live delivery end-to-end)` group (test file ~line 722) drives a REAL losing-local conflict through `updateResource → shouldResolveConflict → _conflictModule.resolveConflict → _emitConflict → onConflict → _conflictController.add` and asserts exactly one `ConflictEvent(chosenStrategy: remoteWon, docId, remoteValue.editCount: 9)` reaches a pre-subscribed `service.conflictStream` listener. That is the exact one-liner the prior entry prescribed; the wire-up is now intent-gated. The file is exemplary: real conflict resolution through `FakeFirebaseFirestore`, side-effects (cache + disk) asserted not just returns, correct local `_MockAuthRepository extends Mock` (shared one's concrete `currentUserId` blocks `when()`), recoverLocalVersion BUT-1263 (stamps `lastEditedBy`+`lastEditedAt`) + BUT-1264 (false-branch of the `remote.editCount > base` guard). Subject is NOT mocked away — only the auth repo (external collaborator) is.
Two REMAINING gaps (both Medium-or-below, neither commit-blocking — file is committed green):
- **`shouldResolveConflict` FALSE branches are untested at the service level** (`conflict_resolution_module.dart:40-58`). All conflict-path service tests drive the TRUE branch (recordLocalUpdate within 5s + remote `lastEditedAt.isAfter(lastUpdate)`). The three falses — (a) no prior `recordLocalUpdate` → `lastUpdate==null` → false; (b) >5000ms since the local update (`conflictResolutionWindowMs`) → false; (c) remote `lastEditedAt` NOT after `lastUpdate` → false — gate whether ANY write enters conflict resolution. A `<`→`<=` window slip, an `isAfter`→`isBefore` flip, or dropping the window check would make every back-to-back owner write spuriously conflict-resolve (or never), and no test in the file goes red. The module is constructor-pure (inject `getLatestResource` closure + `onConflict: emitted.add`) — cheapest to pin at the MODULE level via `withClock` per-call (hit 4999ms then 5000ms, pinning the strict `<`), mirroring the import_rate_limiter window pattern. Medium.
- **`RealtimeSyncService.resolveConflict<T>(T local, T remote)` (lib lines 377-409) is a DEAD DUPLICATE of `ConflictResolutionModule.resolveConflict` that OMITS the `_emitConflict` calls.** Grepped all `resolveConflict` callers: `updateResource` uses `_conflictModule.resolveConflict` (line 260); the viewmodel path (`realtime_recipe_viewmodel.dart:382` → `realtime_recipe_operations.dart:273`) is a DIFFERENT method (`resolveConflict({recipeId, localVersion, remoteVersion, resolution})` on `_editingModule`). Nothing calls the service's public `resolveConflict(local, remote)`. The 4 `resolveConflict (last-write-wins...)` tests (file lines 665-720) pin winner-selection on this dead public method — not WRONG (it's public API, `identical(winner, ...)` is a sound contract pin) but they create a FALSE impression that "conflict resolution is covered" while the actually-used `_conflictModule.resolveConflict` is exercised end-to-end only for the `remoteWon` branch (the single BUT-1265 test). localWon / tie-break-local / tie-break-remote on the EVENT-EMITTING module path have no end-to-end coverage. **Reusable lesson: when a service hand-copies a module's algorithm into a same-named public method, grep for callers before trusting same-named tests — winner-selection tests on the dead copy don't cover the live (event-emitting) copy, and the divergence (no `_emitConflict` on the service copy) is a latent production smell the tests can't surface.** Recommend deleting the dead duplicate (per the 2026-05-25 BUT-1072 "prefer a deletion-PR over a pinning-test" rule) and, if the localWon/tie branches matter to the banner, adding emit-path tests through `updateResource`. Info→Medium.
Did not modify the test file (review-only task). `flutter test` on this file was still in the Windows ~12-min compile floor at report time; the file is committed green in d4ee51b0f.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1038 group multi-select removal [Pattern]
Reviewed a 4-test draft for long-press multi-select removal of group members (GroupMemberCard selection params + GroupMembersList stateful selection + inline "Ta bort markerade (N)" bar). The `_StubPermissionService extends Fake implements PermissionService` (overriding only `currentUserId`/`isGroupAdmin`/`canInviteToGroup`/`isOwner`) + register-in-GetIt + `production.ServiceLocator.initialize(DIContainer())` harness from `group_detail_report_tiles_test.dart` is the right seam — the card/list resolve `PermissionService` via `ServiceLocator.get` only, so NO Firebase Core mock/pigeon handlers needed. **Two meaningful gaps a happy-path-only draft missed (both cheap, pure widget-level, no `removeMultipleMembers`/UnifiedFriendsService stub):** (1) **the `selectable = canRemoveMember` guard** — in selection mode a non-removable member (owner/self) must keep its avatar and ignore taps; if `&& selectable` were dropped from `onTap`/`leading`, the owner could be toggled into the set and fed to bulk-remove, and an all-`'other-uid'` test stays green. Pin with the owner's OWN tile (`uid == currentUserId == ownerId`): `circle_outlined`/`check_circle` findsNothing + tap → toggle callback fires 0×. (2) **clear-on-cancel** — the "bar reveals with count" test stops short; tap `Icons.close` then re-enter selection and assert the count is back to **1, not 2** (proves `_selectedIds.clear()` ran, not just `_selectionMode=false`). Added both → file is 6 tests, all green, analyze clean. **Stopping before the final Remove CTA is an ACCEPTABLE boundary** — that path is `removeMultipleMembers` → confirm dialog → `UnifiedFriendsService`, and the LEAF (`removeFriendFromCategory`) is unit-tested in `friend_categories_operations_test.dart`. **Honest scope note: the `removeMultipleMembers` ORCHESTRATION itself (loop + partial-fail snackbar + `GroupEventBus.memberRemoved` + count return) has NO test — `GroupDetailActions` has zero test files in `test/`** — but that's a pre-existing gap predating this diff (the method shipped under BUT-997), not introduced by the UI change, so it's a backlog item, not a commit blocker.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1080 notifications inbox bulk-dismiss [Pattern + Bug-class]
Reviewed the BUT-1080 boundary (long-press multi-select + bulk dismiss). Repo `deleteByIds(docIds,userId)` is well-covered (4 new cases mirroring `deleteAllByUser`: deletes-only-requested / refuses-cross-user / ignores-foreign-id / empty→0). **The repo-layer coverage was NOT sufficient on its own** — the VM `dismissSelected` carries an invariant the repo can't prove and a VM test was both cheap and warranted, so I ADDED `test/unit/viewmodels/notifications_viewmodel_test.dart` (6 tests, green). Why cheap: `NotificationsViewModel({NotificationService? notificationService})` is constructor-injectable, `MockNotificationService extends Mock` already exists in `production_mocks.dart` as a CLEAN Mock (no concrete `@override` bodies on the methods → `when()` works), the model has a trivial const ctor, and the VM resolves its dep ONLY via the ctor arg → **no DI bridge, no `TestServiceLocator`, no Firebase Core mock needed**. Just `MockNotificationService()` + `when(getNotificationHistory).thenAnswer` to seed `loadHistory()`, then drive `dismissSelected`.
**The load-bearing invariant the repo test can't reach: `dismissSelected` filters `_entries` on `e.id`, while the sibling `markAsOpened` one method up filters on `e.notificationId`.** A copy-paste regression to `e.notificationId` stays green at the repo layer and against any naive happy-path VM test that uses the same string for both. Pin it with entries whose `id` ('a') DIFFERS from `notificationId` ('notif-a'), then assert `dismissSelected({'notif-a'})` is a no-op (0 removed, no service call). Companion tests: selective-removal-leaves-others, forwards-exact-ids (`verify(captureAny).captured.single as List<String>` → `.toSet()`), notifies-listeners, empty-set no-op.
**Bug-class / mocktail trap for testing a fire-and-forget `.catchError` swallow on a `Future<int>` method:** the production code is `service.deleteHistoryNotifications(ids).catchError((e){log; return 0;})`. Modeling the failure with `when(...).thenThrow(...)` makes the mock throw SYNCHRONOUSLY at the call site → `.catchError` is never attached → throw escapes `unawaited()` and the test "fails" misleadingly (this distinguishes a sync-throw from the async rejection production actually faces — the method is `async` so real failures are rejected futures). Next attempt `thenAnswer((_) async => throw ...)` ALSO fails: mocktail erases the type arg to `Future<dynamic>`, tripping `Future.catchError`'s "handler must return the future's type" runtime check (the `return 0` int handler no longer matches dynamic). **Correct model: `thenAnswer((_) => Future<int>.error(Exception(...)))`** — a typed rejected future matching production's `Future<int>`, which `.catchError` swallows cleanly. Assert the optimistic removal STANDS after the failure (entries stay gone; the VM does NOT rollback by design — fire-and-forget, a refresh would resurrect). **General rule: to test a `.catchError`-swallow on a typed-future method, stub with `Future<T>.error(...)`, never `thenThrow` (sync) and never `async => throw` (type-erased).**
**View selection-mode in `notifications_view.dart` left untested — acceptable boundary** per the BUT-1038 pattern + ui-conventions.md (Stateful selection-mode UI is heavy Provider+VM scaffolding for low regression value; the logic worth pinning — selective removal, no-op guards, failure-survival — all lives in the now-tested VM). Service pass-through (`deleteHistoryNotifications` → `_historyRepository.deleteByIds(docIds, _userId)`) is a one-line forward with the security scoping in the repo (tested) — no own test earns its keep.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1194..1198 sprint audit (6 test/lib pairs) [Pattern — all adequate]
Audited a 6-pair sprint; verdict ADEQUATE, nothing blocking. The high-value reusable judgements:
- **`ConfidenceIndicator` (BUT-1196) — the icon-color assertion is correct precisely because production splits alpha.** Production sets `badgeBackgroundColor`/`badgeBorderColor` via `token.withValues(alpha: ...)` but `badgeIconColor = context.butleryColors.success` at FULL opacity. The test captures `tokens.success = context.butleryColors.success` and asserts `icon.color == tokens.success` — matches because it targets the un-alpha'd icon token, NOT the alpha'd background. **If a future review wants to also pin the background it must capture `token.withValues(alpha: AppDimensions.opacityVeryLight)`, not the bare token.** Threshold boundaries pinned at the exact `>=` edges (0.8 / 0.6 / 0.59) — that's the load-bearing part (a `>` vs `>=` regression flips a bucket). `floors rather than rounds (0.999→99%)` pins the `.toInt()` truncation contract a future `.round()` swap would break. Captures tokens via `Builder` — theme-safe. Adequate.
- **`getCommentLikers` (BUT-1194) — correctly on the FAKE lane, not the emulator lane, despite the repo file using `FieldValue.increment`/`serverTimestamp` elsewhere.** Decision rule confirmed: lane choice keys on what the METHOD UNDER TEST executes, not what sibling methods in the same file use. `getCommentLikers` is a plain `orderBy('likedAt', descending:true).limit().get()` — fake-runnable. The test SIDESTEPS the fake's serverTimestamp-ordering weakness by seeding explicit `Timestamp.fromDate(...)` values (documented in the header) — the right call, since `toggleCommentLike`'s production write stamps `serverTimestamp()` which a fake can't order across rapid writes. desc-order + limit-keeps-most-recent + default-100 + empty→[] is the full contract. Adequate; emulator lane would be over-engineering here.
- **`smart_import_view_smoke_test` (BUT-1197) — a smoke/mount test that EARNS its keep because the risk it guards is real-and-narrow: "does the 467-line `State.build` split still resolve its self-built `ChangeNotifierProvider(create: ServiceLocator.get<ImportManager>())`?"** The `find.byType(TextField), findsWidgets` assert is load-bearing, not topology-rot: it proves `build()` reached the real body (line 624 TextField) rather than an ErrorWidget — i.e. the DI graph resolved AND the post-frame clipboard-read initState didn't throw. This is the BUT-368-permitted exception: a mount guard for a freshly-split large view with a non-trivial DI `create:`, not a `findsOneWidget` on `ChangeNotifierProvider<VM>`. The clipboard stub returning `{'text':''}` is necessary (empty → no URL → no suggestion dialog → clean settle). Adequate.
- **`OcrErrorMessageBuilder` (BUT-1195) — the empty-list-vs-absent-key distinction is the real win.** The `recommendations: <String>[]` case (non-null but `.isNotEmpty==false`) is a branch the transitive VM-level `contains` suite never isolated; pairing `contains(ocrQualityTips)` WITH `isNot(contains(labelImprovementSuggestions))` pins mutual-exclusivity so a refactor collapsing the if/else can't pass. `_result` using the FULL ctor (not `.failure`, which hard-codes confidence 0.0) so the `confidence: 0.42` field assert isn't vacuous — documented and correct. Adequate.
- **`AllergenMismatch` (BUT-1198) — exemplary pure-detector coverage.** FREE-ignored / UNKNOWN-ignored / tracked-not-flagged / no-tagResult-empty are the four safety-relevant negative-space cases (a regression that flagged FREE or UNKNOWN would spam the banner; one that ignored an untracked CONTAINS would hide a real allergen). multi-allergen confirms set-builder accumulation. Nothing to add.
- **`persistence_service` scroll-offset (BUT-1028) — default-0.0 / round-trip / overwrite is the complete contract** for a get/set pair over SharedPreferences. (Sidebar: the file's `TestMockSharedPreferences` is unused dead scaffolding by these 3 tests — they use `SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues` — but that's pre-existing file cruft, not introduced here, so not a finding.)
**Two NON-gaps confirmed acceptable as follow-ups, NOT blockers:** (1) `mina_recept_view.dart` ScrollController wiring (BUT-1028 view layer) — the persistable LOGIC (offset get/set/default) is unit-tested; the ScrollController→`setRecipeListScrollOffset(controller.offset)` debounce + `jumpTo(savedOffset)` restore is view-layer plumbing covered by journey/manual passes per ui-conventions.md, and a multi-scrollable view's controller wiring is exactly the fragile-finder territory the 2026-06-03 BUT-1180 entry warns against testing structurally. (2) `allergen_setup_banner.dart` MaterialBanner — `ScaffoldMessenger.showMaterialBanner` fire-and-forget is the same untestable-in-isolation shape as a SnackBar; the testable unit (the allergen-set computation) IS tested via `AllergenMismatch`. **General rule reaffirmed: when a feature splits into a pure detector + a ScaffoldMessenger/ScrollController presentation shell, test the detector exhaustively and let the shell ride the journey/manual lane — do NOT manufacture a structural widget test to chase the line.** No weak/proves-nothing tests found; no subject-mocked-away tests found; commit is clear from the testing-specialist's side.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1199 SwipeHintBanner generalization audit [Pattern — adequate, one strengthening]
A self-contained once-per-device hint widget was parameterized (`seenKey`/`icon`/`message`; static `seenKey`→`recipeSwipeSeenKey`, two new keys `cookingStepSeenKey`/`shoppingClaimSeenKey`). Verdict ADEQUATE, not blocking. Reusable judgements:
- **The dismiss-side isolation test is the load-bearing one and it's correct: `expect(prefs.getBool(shoppingClaimSeenKey), isTrue)` paired with `expect(prefs.getBool(recipeSwipeSeenKey), isNull)`.** `isNull` not `isFalse` is the right matcher — it proves the recipe key was NEVER WRITTEN, not merely "is false." This is the flip-detection that fails if `_dismiss` ever wrote the static key instead of `widget.seenKey`. Pattern: **for a parameterized-key persistence widget, the dismiss test must assert the OTHER keys are `isNull` (untouched), not just that the own key is `isTrue`.**
- **WEAKNESS caught (read-side isolation, test c "honours its own seenKey"): asserting only `findsOneWidget` on the cooking icon when the recipe key is flagged is the POSITIVE half only.** It catches the primary regression (recipe key bleeding into the cooking banner → cooking goes `findsNothing`) but NOT "ignores the flag entirely / always shows." **Strengthening rule: an isolation test needs BOTH halves in the SAME prefs state — the default banner under the recipe key must go `findsNothing` AND the parameterized banner must still `findsOneWidget`.** Recommended as polish, not a gate, because the worst regression is still caught by the positive half.
- **Test-message literal `'Long-press a step'` (not a real l10n key) in the unit test is CORRECT** — decouples the widget-unit test from ARB copy; the real `context.l10n.cookingStepHintText`/`shoppingClaimHintText` wiring rides the call sites. Verified both view insertions (`cooking_mode_view.dart`, `collaborative_shopping_items.dart`) pass distinct correct `seenKey`/icon/`context.l10n.*`, and the recipe default site stayed `const SwipeHintBanner()` so the backward-compat default path the original 3 tests cover is real.
- **View-placement of an already-tested widget is manual/journey territory (ui-conventions), not a widget-unit gap** — same ruling as the 2026-06-04 BUT-1194 sprint. Confirmed l10n keys resolve (no missing-key runtime risk). The icon+message render test (a) asserting BOTH `find.byIcon` AND `find.text` (not just that it builds) is the right depth — proves the params reach the rendered Row.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1037 recipe-likeness gate audit [Pattern — adequate, no blocker]
A pure `RecipeTextHeuristic.looksLikeRecipe(String)` LLM-cost gate (`measures>=1 && verbs>=1` OR `measures+verbs>=3`, with `\b`-anchored unit regex + word-prefix verb regex) wired into a view dialog + a thin analytics method. 9-case test. Verdict ADEQUATE, not blocking. Reusable judgements:
- **The load-bearing false-positive guard is "does not match units inside other words."** Verified by tracing the regex: `\b(...g...|...l...)\b` does NOT fire on the `g` in "good"/"great" or `l` in "loaf" (no word boundary inside the word), so measures=0, only the `bake` verb hits → false. **This assertion genuinely fails if someone drops the `\b` anchors** — it's the real guard, not topology rot. For any heuristic with single-letter/short tokens (`g`/`l`/`dl`/`st`), the "substring-inside-word must NOT count" negative case is the highest-value test and must be present.
- **The verb-less count-branch boundary case is the other must-have.** `'200 g\n3 dl\n... 5 dl'` → measures=3, verbs=0, hits `measures+verbs>=3` at the exact `>=` edge. It's the ONLY branch that admits an ingredient-only paste; without it that path is untested. Pin it at exactly 3 hits (the boundary), as the test does.
- **Don't flag a passing-via-the-other-branch test as vacuous if its INTENT still flips on a real regression.** The "EN metric (g not cups)" case passes via the AND-branch (`g`+`ml`+`bake`); `g` isn't strictly load-bearing (drop it, `ml`+`bake` still passes), but the test still flips if the EN verb list or `ml` unit is removed — intent (EN recipes survive) is real. Keep.
- **A thin analytics passthrough (`logWarnDialogCancelled` → `logEvent(name, {'source': source})`, no branching) and a `showDialog`-driven view gate (`_parseAndNavigate`/`_confirmNonRecipeImport`) are NOT unit gaps** — journey/manual territory per BUT-387 Phase 6 + ui-conventions. The pure function is the entire testable surface and it's covered. Confirmed `looksLikeRecipe` is the only seam with branching/regression risk; the dialog + emitter ride the journey lane.
- **Optional strengthening (P3, not a gate): a CHARACTERIZATION test pinning the gate's intended LENIENCY** — non-recipe prose with one standalone unit + one verb-prefix word (e.g. `'I weigh 80 kg and I cook dinner most evenings.'`) returns `true`. This is inherent to a cheap heuristic (user can back out of the warn dialog), so pin it as a documented-limitation so a future "tighten the gate" change has to consciously update it rather than silently breaking the override-UX assumption. Same `CHARACTERIZATION:`-prefix pattern as the 2026-05-25 YouTubeTranscriptService entry.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-905 SemanticsService.announce — conscious skip [Pattern — no test]
Two `SemanticsService.announce(...)` calls added inline: favorite-toggle in `recipe_detail_view.dart` (`isFavorite ? a11yRecipeFavorited : a11yRecipeUnfavorited`) and comment-post in `recipe_detail/handlers/recipe_social_handler.dart` (`a11yCommentPosted`). **Verdict: conscious skip, no widget test.** Reusable ruling for `SemanticsService.announce` side effects:
- **`announce` is fire-and-forget with NO observable widget-tree effect** — the only way to assert it fired is intercepting `SystemChannels.accessibility`, which is brittle + high-harness-cost (needs mocked self-loading VM + recipe + l10n scaffold + finding an `IconButton` in a `SliverAppBar`). This is exactly the "skip tests needing heavy ViewModel scaffolding — covered by audit script + manual screen-reader passes" carve-out in `ui-conventions.md`.
- **The announce lives inside an inline `onPressed`/handler closure** → a channel-interception test pins call-site topology, not contract. The *correct* refactor (extracting the favorite handler the way the comment announce already lives in a handler) would break the test with zero behaviour change = BUT-368 topology-rot anti-pattern.
- **The `favorited/unfavorited` ternary is not a domain branch worth pinning** — it reads VM state the toggle already mutated; the sole failure mode is "two l10n keys swapped," which a screen-reader pass catches instantly. No aggregation/chunking/permission/locale invariant in play.
- **Real (free) regression guard = the l10n keys.** `a11yRecipeFavorited` / `a11yRecipeUnfavorited` / `a11yCommentPosted` must exist in BOTH `app_sv.arb` + `app_en.arb` with `flutter gen-l10n` run; a missing key is a compile error `flutter analyze` already gates. The existing per-chunk `find.bySemanticsLabel` tests cover *labels*; announcement interception is a deliberate, documented convention gap, not an oversight.
- **General rule going forward: a `SemanticsService.announce(...)` added to a view/handler is manual/a11y-pass territory, not a widget-unit gap** — same family as the BUT-1194/BUT-1037 view-placement rulings above.

### 2026-06-13 — BUT-1245 pure-threshold extraction pattern [Pattern discovered]
**When a static method mixes pure threshold decisions with DI/dialog calls, extract the decisions as `@visibleForTesting` statics rather than building a DI bridge or widget pump.** `ImportResultHandler.checkForDuplicates` owned two boundaries — `score >= 0.6` (fingerprint match gate) and `score < 0.8 ? 0.8 : score` (display-score floor) — that needed zero services, no `BuildContext`, no dialog. Extracting them as `meetsContentDuplicateThreshold(double)` and `clampToExactMatchFloor(double)` and having the parent call them is a pure refactor (the inlined expressions are word-for-word identical), but it opens a one-import test file with 9 tests and no DI scaffolding at all. **When to use this pattern**: the logic is a predicate or transformation over primitives (doubles, ints, strings), the threshold constants are `static const`, and the decision step is surrounded by side-effect code that makes the method untestable whole. **Test file pattern**: two `group()` blocks — one per helper — each with exact-boundary, just-below-boundary, well-above, and zero/sentinel cases. Each test has a comment-sentence explaining which regression it guards against (`>= vs >`, floor removal, over-aggressive clamping). No imports beyond `flutter_test` and the production file. File lives at `test/unit/views/<subdirectory>/<helper>_test.dart` (not `test/views/`). **Gotcha: `@visibleForTesting` on an `abstract final class` static is available from `package:flutter/material.dart`** (re-exports `foundation.dart`); no extra import needed as long as the production file already imports Material. Analyze confirmed clean, arch test confirmed unaffected, 9/9 new + 73 existing tests green.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1079 pt1 read-only SourceArtefact sheet — CONSCIOUS SKIP [Pattern — no test]
`recipe_detail_view.dart` adds an overflow item → `showModalBottomSheet` displaying a persisted `SourceArtefact`: a type label via a private exhaustive `_sourceTypeLabel` `switch` over 6 `SourceArtefactType` values, a captured-at via `ContextualTimeFormatter.standard`, and a scrollable `SelectableText` of the raw payload. No mutation, no service call, no branch beyond the exhaustive switch. **Verdict: conscious skip — same family as BUT-918 / BUT-946 static-disclosure rulings.** Reusable judgements:
- **An exhaustive `switch` over an enum is its OWN completeness guarantee** — a missing arm is a `flutter analyze` error (the test never runs). A widget test pinning "`url` → the SV URL string" only hardcodes the l10n VALUE, duplicating `gen-l10n` with a brittle copy that breaks on a harmless wording tweak = the BUT-918 "literal string renders" anti-pattern. A *wrong* mapping is a copy defect for a screen-reader/manual pass, not a behavioural regression.
- **The "read-only sheet does no mutation/re-extract" safety test is a BUT-946 tautology** — the re-extract path DOESN'T EXIST YET (prod comment: "Re-extract … is a follow-up"). Bypass/no-mutation tests are only worth writing once the guarded path exists; it'll arrive with its own intent + tests.
- **Both null-guards are already correct (item gated on `sourceArtefact != null` AND handler re-checks)** — a code-review observation, not a test. No null-deref surface.
- **Harness cost fails the second half of the decision rule**: `RecipeDetailView` self-loads via VM through the production-ServiceLocator bridge; driving the sheet needs mocked VM + bridge + `createLocalizedTestApp` + a seeded recipe with a non-null artefact + pumping past the self-load gate + opening the overflow `PopupMenu` (which overflows its Row on the 800px default surface → needs the scoped `FlutterError.onError` 'A RenderFlex overflowed' ignore from the BUT-1180 files). High cost, zero coupling to any of it.
- **If a recipe-detail JOURNEY test ever lands in `test/views/`, the sheet is one beat there** (open overflow → View captured source → type+timestamp+payload visible), alongside the future re-extract flow. Not a standalone mechanical widget test now.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-904 AutoSaveManager<T> primitive extraction — ADEQUATE [Pattern — no blocker]
Shared draft-persistence primitive (`lib/services/persistence/auto_save_manager.dart`) extracted from 5 per-surface `_load/_save/_clearDraft` try/catch triads; 3 surfaces migrated (URL import, text import, comment composer). Deliberately NOT a BaseService/DI singleton — widget/VM-owned, single storage key, disposed with owner. 13-test unit suite + the EXISTING comment widget test passing UNMODIFIED. Verdict ADEQUATE. Reusable judgements:
- **For a `<T>` over `SharedPreferences`, the `@visibleForTesting Future<SharedPreferences> Function()? prefsProvider` ctor seam is the right best-effort-error harness** — pass `() => Future.error(StateError(...))` to prove `load` returns null / `save`/`clear`/`flush` `completes` without propagating. This is exactly the contract the extraction EXISTS to centralize (each removed copy had its own swallow-to-`AppLogger.warning`); without an injected-throw test the "best-effort" promise is unproven. Pin it.
- **The load-bearing domain invariant is "encode-to-empty / encode-null ⇒ REMOVE the key, never write \"\"."** All 3 surfaces rely on it (delete-everything-in-the-field must drop the draft, not persist an empty string that re-seeds a blank draft on next mount). Two tests cover it (empty-string-encodes-empty, encoder-returns-null) — both genuinely flip if `_write` drops the `encoded.isEmpty` branch. Highest-value tests in the suite; an LLM-draft "save persists the value" alone would miss this.
- **An EXISTING widget test passing UNMODIFIED across a primitive swap IS the migration's behaviour-preservation gate** — `comment_form_widget_test.dart` drives the real `comment_draft_v1_<recipeId>` keys (load-on-mount, save-on-keystroke, clear-on-post, per-recipe isolation) through the new manager. No new widget test needed for the comment surface; the green unmodified test proves the refactor is contract-preserving. Don't add a duplicate manager-level test for what the widget test already exercises end-to-end.
- **debounce/flush are tested but NOT exercised by any current surface** (all 3 migrate with `debounce: Duration.zero` eager). Acceptable forward-looking coverage, not impl-coupling: they're published contract of the primitive and BUT-910 (photo import) is the named intended adopter. The generic `<T>` JSON-map round-trip likewise justifies the abstraction existing over a String-only helper — keep it.
- **FOLLOW-UP GAP (non-blocking, file as ticket): the two import views have NO draft-persistence widget test.** Grepped `test/` for `url_import_draft|text_import_draft|ImportViaUrl|FranSocialaMedier` → zero hits. The comment surface got its end-to-end gate (BUT-1058); the URL + text import surfaces rely on the manager unit tests + manual verification only. A `tester.enterText → prefs key written` + `seed prefs → field restored on mount` widget pair per view would mirror the comment gate. Not a commit blocker (primitive is unit-proven, migration is mechanical, no behaviour change in the diff), but a real coverage gap for those two surfaces' load/save/clear wiring.

### 2026-06-04 — BUT-1203 group-draft codec-extraction gate — SOUND, no blocker [Pattern]
Group-creation draft migrated from inline `_load/_save/_clearDraft` JSON triad → `AutoSaveManager<Map<String,dynamic>>`. The NOVEL logic (the all-fields-empty → null/remove-key rule + the JSON shape) was extracted into a PURE codec `lib/widgets/social/groups/group_draft_codec.dart` (`buildGroupDraft`/`encodeGroupDraft`/`decodeGroupDraft`) and gated by 7 unit tests. Verdict: the codec test genuinely proves the migration's behaviour-preservation contract; gating via the pure codec instead of a full-widget pump is the CORRECT call, not a shortcut. Reusable judgements:
- **When a widget-draft migration introduces NEW persistence logic, extract the novel decision (emptiness rule + wire shape) into a pure function and gate THAT.** The dialog's residual glue (load→setState + friend-id resolution) is mechanical and shape-identical to 3 already-shipped string migrations — re-pumping the full dialog (provider + l10n + friends-service scaffolding) to re-prove it would be the BUT-368 structural-pump anti-pattern, high cost, near-zero marginal coverage. The codec seam is the right seam.
- **The load-bearing invariant `all-empty ⇒ null ⇒ remove-key` is what survives the refactor and is directly tested** (incl. the absent-fields `{}` defensive case). It flips iff the emptiness rule breaks. Plus a **byte-compat test decoding the EXACT legacy `_saveDraft` JSON blob** (`{"name":...,"emoji":"📚","friendIds":["x"]}`) — this is the migration's real risk (a key-name or shape drift would silently orphan every pre-migration persisted draft, since the SharedPreferences key is kept byte-identical). That test is the single most load-bearing one in the file; keep it.
